###### Art. 451

When two or more persons have participated in a robbery or other crime, all shall be responsible for the murder committed on this ground or occasion; unless it is proven who committed it, and that the others took no part in it, nor could remedy or prevent it.

Jurisprudencia:
•	Gaceta Judicial, COAUTORIA, 24-jun-1977
•	Gaceta Judicial, COAUTORIA, 07-jun-1983

###### Art. 452

Those who knowingly and voluntarily kill any ascendant or descendant, spouse or sibling, shall be punished with special major imprisonment of sixteen to twenty-five years.

Nota: Artículo reformado por Decreto Supremo 2636, publicado en el Registro Oficial 621 de 4 de Julio de 1978 .  
Nota: Decreto Supremo 2636 derogado por Decreto Legislativo s/n, publicado en el Registro Oficial 36 de 1 de octubre de 1979 , que ordena volver al texto legal anterior. Nota: Artículo sustituido por Ley No. 105, publicada en Registro Oficial 365 de 21 de Julio de 1998 .  
Nota: Artículo reformado por Ley No. 47, publicada en Registro Oficial 422 de 28 de Septiembre del 2001 .  

Concordancias:

•	CODIGO CIVIL (

## LIBRO III)

, Arts. 1010

Jurisprudencia:

•	Gaceta Judicial, DELITO DE UXORICIDIO A SU CONYUGE, 17-ago-2007

###### Art. 453

The mother who, to conceal her dishonor, kills her newborn child, shall be punished with the penalty of minor imprisonment of three to six years.  
The same penalty shall be imposed on the maternal grandparents who, to conceal the mother's dishonor, commit this crime.  

Concordancias:

•	CONSTITUCION DE LA REPUBLICA DEL ECUADOR, Arts. 66, 44, 45

###### Art. 454

Anyone who instigates or provides assistance to another to commit suicide shall be punished with imprisonment of one to four years and a fine of eight to seventy-seven dollars of the United States of North America, if the suicide has been attempted or consummated.

Nota: Artículo reformado por Art. 126 de Ley No. 75, publicada en Registro Oficial 635 de 7 de Agosto del 2002 .  

Jurisprudencia:
•	Gaceta Judicial, SUICIDIO, 30-jul-1903

###### Art. 455

When wounds or blows, inflicted voluntarily, but without intent to kill, have caused death, the offender shall be punished with three to six years of minor imprisonment.  
They shall be punished with minor imprisonment of six to nine years, if they committed these acts of violence with any of the circumstances detailed in Art. 450.  

Jurisprudencia:

•	Gaceta Judicial, HERIDAS Y GOLPES QUE CAUSAN MUERTE SIN INTENCION, 30-sep-1987

###### Art. 456

If substances administered voluntarily, which can seriously harm health, have been given without intent to cause death, but have produced it, the guilty party shall be punished with minor imprisonment of three to six years.

###### Art. 457

In the offense mentioned in the preceding article, the intention to kill shall be presumed if the person who administered the harmful substances is a physician, pharmacist or chemist; or if he possesses knowledge in said professions, even though he does not have the titles or diplomas to practice them.

###### Art. 458

In the cases mentioned in Arts. 454, 455 and 456, if the guilty party has committed the offense against his father or other ascendant, or descendant, spouse or sibling, the minimum of the penalties established in said articles shall be increased by two additional years.

###### Art. 459

He is guilty of unintentional homicide who has caused the harm through lack of foresight or precaution, but without intention to harm another.  

Jurisprudencia:
•	Gaceta Judicial, HOMICIDIO INTENCIONAL, 18-nov-1949
•	Gaceta Judicial, HOMICIDIO ININTENCIONAL, 16-mar-1983

•	Gaceta Judicial, HOMICIDIO ININTENCIONAL POR NEGLIGENCIA MEDICA, 05-mar-2001

###### Art. 460

Whoever unintentionally has caused the death of another person, if the act is not more severely punished, shall be penalized with imprisonment of three months to two years and a fine of eight to thirty-one dollars of the United States of North America.  
Nota: Artículo reformado por Art. 127 de Ley No. 75, publicada en Registro Oficial 635 de 7 de Agosto del 2002 .  

Jurisprudencia:

•	Gaceta Judicial, DELITO DE ESTAFA, 17-sep-1998
•	Gaceta Judicial, CAMBIO DE LA INFRACCION ACUSADA EN EL AUTO DE LLAMAMIENTO A PLENARIO, 17-mar-2004

###### Art. 461

When in a fight or assault in which more than two persons take part, a death results, without it being established who caused it, all those who exercised violence upon the person of the victim shall be held as authors, and the penalty of one to five years of imprisonment and a fine of thirty-one to seventy-seven dollars of the United States of North America shall be applied.  

Nota: Artículo reformado por Art. 128 de Ley No. 75, publicada en Registro Oficial 635 de 7 de Agosto del 2002 .

###### Art. 462

Homicide caused by an athlete, in the act of a sport and against the person of another athlete in play, shall not be punished when it clearly appears that there was no intention nor violation of the respective regulations, and provided that it involves a sport not prohibited in the Republic.  

In the contrary case, the general rules of this Chapter on homicide shall apply.  

Jurisprudencia:
•	Gaceta Judicial, DESAFIO DEL BOX, 22-jun-1936

#### CAPITULO II

Of injuries

###### Art. 463

Whoever wounds or strikes another, causing him an illness or incapacity for personal work, that exceeds three days and does not exceed eight, shall be punished with imprisonment of fifteen days to three months and a fine of six to twelve dollars of the United States of North America.  
If any of the circumstances of Art. 450 is present, the penalties shall be imprisonment of two to

six months and a fine of eight to sixteen dollars of the United States of North America.  

Nota: Artículo reformado por Art. 129 de Ley No. 75, publicada en Registro Oficial 635 de 7 de Agosto del 2002 .  

Concordancias:
•	CODIGO DE PROCEDIMIENTO PENAL 2000, Arts. 105
•	CODIGO DEL TRABAJO, Arts. 362

Jurisprudencia:

•	Gaceta Judicial, INCAPACIDAD PARA EL TRABAJO, 09-jun-1916
•	Gaceta Judicial, LESIONES Y HERIDAS, 01-jul-1948
•	Gaceta Judicial, LESIONES, 04-abr-1997
•	Gaceta Judicial, LESIONES, 16-feb-2009
•	Gaceta Judicial, GOLPES O HERIDAS, 17-feb-2009
•	Gaceta Judicial, LESIONES, 10-mar-2009

###### Art. 464

If the blows or wounds have caused an illness or an incapacity for personal work, that exceeds eight days and does not exceed one month, the penalties shall be imprisonment of two months to one year and a fine of twelve to thirty-one dollars of the United States of North America.  

If any of the circumstances of Art. 450 is present, the imprisonment shall be six months to two years, and the fine, sixteen to forty-seven dollars of the United States of North America.  

Nota: Artículo reformado por Art. 130 de Ley No. 75, publicada en Registro Oficial 635 de 7 de Agosto del 2002 .  

Concordancias:

•	CODIGO DEL TRABAJO, Arts. 362

Jurisprudencia:
•	Gaceta Judicial, DELITO DE HERIDAS, 07-sep-1953
•	Gaceta Judicial, JUICIO PENAL POR LESIONES, 19-oct-1953
•	Gaceta Judicial, HERIDAS, 03-oct-1960
•	Gaceta Judicial, DELITO DE GOLPES Y HERIDAS, 28-sep-1964
•	Gaceta Judicial, DELITO POR LESIONES, 12-ene-1995
•	Gaceta Judicial, DELITO DE LESIONES, 27-nov-1998

•	Gaceta Judicial, HERIDAS O LESIONES, 10-feb-2009

###### Art. 465

If the blows or wounds have caused an illness or incapacity for work, that exceeds thirty days and does not exceed ninety, the penalties shall be imprisonment of six months to two years, and a fine of sixteen to forty-seven dollars of the United States of North America.  
In case any of the circumstances of Art. 450 is present, the imprisonment shall be one to three years, and the fine sixteen to sixty-two dollars of the United States of North America.  

Nota: Artículo reformado por Art. 131 de Ley No. 75, publicada en Registro Oficial 635 de 7 de Agosto del 2002 .  

Concordancias:

•	CODIGO DEL TRABAJO, Arts. 362

###### Art. 466

If the blows or wounds have caused an illness or incapacity for personal work, that exceeds ninety days, or a permanent incapacity for the work to which the victim had habitually dedicated himself until then, or a serious illness, or the loss of a non-principal organ, the penalties shall be imprisonment of one to three years and a fine of sixteen to seventy-seven dollars of the United States of North America.  

In case any of the circumstances of Art. 450 is present, the penalties shall be imprisonment of two to five years and a fine of thirty-one to one hundred twenty-five dollars of the United States of North America.  

Nota: Artículo reformado por Art. 132 de Ley No. 75, publicada en Registro Oficial 635 de 7 de Agosto del 2002 .

###### Art. 467

The penalties shall be imprisonment of two to five years and a fine of thirty-one to one hundred twenty-five dollars of the United States of North America, if from the blows or wounds there has resulted a certain or probably incurable illness, or a permanent incapacity for work, or a serious mutilation, or the loss or disablement of a principal organ.  
The penalties shall be minor imprisonment of three to six years and a fine of sixteen to one hundred fifty-six dollars of the United States of North America, if any of the circumstances of Art. 450 is present.  
Nota: Artículo reformado por Art. 133 de Ley No. 75, publicada en Registro Oficial 635 de 7 de Agosto del 2002 .  

Jurisprudencia:

•	Gaceta Judicial, CUERPO DEL DELITO, 19-sep-1932
•	Gaceta Judicial, COARTADA, 31-ago-1976
•	Gaceta Judicial, COARTADA, 31-ene-1989

###### Art. 468

He shall be punished with imprisonment of one to six months and a fine of twelve to thirty-one dollars of the United States of North America, who has caused another an illness or transitory incapacity for personal work, by voluntarily administering to him substances that can seriously alter his health.  

Nota: Artículo reformado por Art. 134 de Ley No. 75, publicada en Registro Oficial 635 de 7 de Agosto del 2002 .

###### Art. 469

The penalty shall be imprisonment of two to five years, when said substances have caused a certain or probably incurable illness, or a permanent incapacity for personal work, or the absolute loss, or disablement of an organ.  

Concordancias:

•	CODIGO DEL TRABAJO, Arts. 360, 361


This excerpt sits under: CODIGO PENAL > LIBRO III)

###### Art. 470

When in a fight or assault in which more than two persons take part, injuries or wounds result, without it being established who caused them, all those who exercised violence upon the person of the victim shall be deemed authors, and the penalty of fifteen days to one year of imprisonment and a fine of eight to sixteen dollars of the United States of North America shall be applied.

Note: Article amended by Art. 135 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 471

In the crimes mentioned in the preceding articles of this Chapter, if the guilty party has committed the offense upon the person of the father or mother or other ascendant or descendant, upon the spouse or upon a sibling, the immediately higher penalty shall be applied.

###### Art. 472

He who has caused unintentional injuries or wounds through lack of foresight or precaution is guilty of unintentional injuries or wounds, and shall be punished with imprisonment of eight days to three months and a fine of six to twelve dollars of the United States of North America, if the act is not more severely punished as a special crime.

Note: Article amended by Art. 136 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 473

In the circumstances of Art. 462, when dealing with injuries or wounds, what is established therein shall apply.

Art. ...- The inciters or those responsible for acts of violence within sports venues or in their vicinity or other places that by their capacity may host mass gatherings of the public, before, during or after the sporting event, artistic or other spectacles and that cause injuries to third parties, shall be punished with a penalty of one to three years of imprisonment.

Note: Article added by Law No. 63, published in Official Register 412 of December 7, 2006.

#### CAPITULO III

Del abandono de personas

###### Art. 474

Those who have abandoned or caused to be abandoned a child in a non-solitary place shall be punished with imprisonment of one month to one year and a fine of six dollars of the United States of North America; and those who have exposed or caused to be exposed the child, provided it is not in a hospice or foundling home.

Note: Article amended by Art. 137 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

Concordancias:

•	CONSTITUCION DE LA REPUBLICA DEL ECUADOR, Arts. 45, 66

###### Art. 475

The crimes provided for in the preceding article shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to two years and a fine of six to sixteen dollars of the United States of North America, if they have been committed by the parents, or by persons to whom the child was entrusted.

Note: Article amended by Art. 138 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 476

If, as a consequence of the abandonment, the child is left mutilated or injured, the guilty parties shall be punished:

In the case provided for by Art. 474, with imprisonment of three months to two years and a fine of eight to thirty-one dollars of the United States of North America; and,

In that of Art. 475, with imprisonment of two to five years and a fine of sixteen to forty-seven dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 139 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 477

If the abandonment has caused the death of the child, the penalty shall be: In the case of Art. 474, imprisonment of one to three years; and in the case of Art. 475, imprisonment of five years.

###### Art. 478

Those who have abandoned or caused to be abandoned a child in a solitary place shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to three years.

###### Art. 479

The imprisonment shall be two to five years, if those guilty of abandonment in a solitary place are the parents, or persons to whom the child was entrusted.

###### Art. 480

If, as a consequence of the abandonment, the child is left injured or mutilated, the guilty party shall be punished with the maximum of the penalties indicated in the two preceding articles.

If the abandonment has caused death, in the case of Art. 478, the penalty shall be lesser imprisonment of three to six years; and in the case of Art. 479, that of greater imprisonment of four to eight years.

#### CAPITULO IV

Del duelo

Note: Chapter amended by Art. 140 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.
Note: Chapter repealed by Law No. 0, published in Official Register Supplement 555 of March 24, 2009.

#### CAPITULO V

Del abuso de armas

###### Art. 488

He who fires a firearm at a person, or assaults him with any other weapon, without wounding him, shall be punished with imprisonment of two years to five years, provided the act does not constitute an attempt.

Note: Article amended by Law No. 31, published in Official Register 231 of March 17, 2006.

Concordancias:

## LIBRO II)

, Arts. 973

### TITULO VII

DE LOS DELITOS CONTRA LA HONRA

CAPITULO UNICO
De la injuria

###### Art. 489.- La injuria es

Calumniosa, cuando consiste en la falsa imputación de un delito; y,
No calumniosa, cuando consiste en toda otra expresión proferida en descrédito, deshonra o menosprecio de otra persona, o en cualquier acción ejecutada con el mismo objeto.

Concordancias:
•	CONSTITUCION DE LA REPUBLICA DEL ECUADOR, Arts. 66
•	CODIGO CIVIL (

## LIBRO IV)

, Arts. 2231
•	CODIGO DE PROCEDIMIENTO PENAL 2000, Arts. 36

•	Gaceta Judicial, INJURIA, 23-jul-1923
•	Gaceta Judicial, INJURIA NO CALUMNIOSA GRAVE, 11-abr-1978
•	Gaceta Judicial, INJURIA CALUMNIOSA, 28-may-1982
•	Gaceta Judicial, ELEMENTOS DEL DELITO DE INJURIA, 01-sep-1999
•	Gaceta Judicial, COMPENSACION DE INJURIAS, 03-oct-2002
•	Gaceta Judicial, INJURIAS, 15-nov-2007
•	Gaceta Judicial, INJURIA CALUMNIOSA, 08-may-2009
•	Gaceta Judicial, CASACION POR INJURIA, 01-jul-2009
•	Gaceta Judicial, INJURIA, 14-jul-2009
•	Gaceta Judicial, FALSA IMPUTACION DE UN DELITO, 23-nov-2009

###### Art. 490.- Las injurias no calumniosas son graves o leves

Son graves:
1o.- La imputación de un vicio o falta de moralidad cuyas consecuencias pueden perjudicar considerablemente la fama, crédito, o intereses del agraviado;
2o.- Las imputaciones que, por su naturaleza, ocasión o circunstancia, fueren tenidas en el concepto público por afrentosas;
3o.- Las imputaciones que racionalmente merezcan la calificación de graves, atendido el estado, dignidad y circunstancias del ofendido y del ofensor; y,
4o.- Las bofetadas, puntapiés, u otras ultrajes de obra.

Son leves las que consisten en atribuir a otro, hechos, apodos o defectos físicos o morales, que no comprometan la honra del injuriado.

Concordancias:

•	CODIGO CIVIL (

## LIBRO I)

, Arts. 352

Jurisprudencia:

•	Gaceta Judicial, INJURIA GRAVE NO CALUMNIOSA, 19-ene-1999
•	Gaceta Judicial, INJURIAS NO CALUMNIOSAS, 12-feb-2009

###### Art. 491

The person guilty of slanderous defamation shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to two years and a fine of six to twenty-five dollars of the United States of North America, when the imputations have been made:

In public meetings or places;
In the presence of ten or more individuals;
By means of writings, printed or not, images or emblems fixed, distributed or sold, placed for sale, or exposed to public view; or,
By means of unpublished writings, but directed or communicated to other persons, including among these letters.

Note: Article amended by Art. 142 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

Concordancias:

•	CODIGO CIVIL (

## LIBRO III)

, Arts. 1231, 1233

Jurisprudencia:
•	Gaceta Judicial, DELITO DE INJURIAS CALUMNIOSAS, 14-nov-1966
•	Gaceta Judicial, INJURIAS CALUMNIOSAS, 06-feb-2009
•	Gaceta Judicial, INJURIA PENAL, 23-sep-2010

###### Art. 492

Those who make the imputation privately, or in the presence of fewer than ten persons, shall be punished with one to six months of imprisonment and a fine of six to twelve dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 143 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 493

Those who have directed to the authority imputations that constitute slanderous insult shall be punished with one to three years of imprisonment and a fine of six to twenty-five dollars of the United States of North America.

If the imputations made to the authority constitute non-slanderous but serious insults, the penalties shall be imprisonment of six months to two years and a fine of six to nineteen dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 144 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 494

Those who have brought a judicial accusation, or made a denunciation, that has not been proven during the trial, shall be punished with imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of six to thirty-one dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 145 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

Concordances:
•	CODE OF CRIMINAL PROCEDURE 2000, Arts. 46, 51, 61

Jurisprudence:

•	Judicial Gazette, RECKLESS OR SLANDEROUS PRIVATE ACCUSATION, 17-Apr-1998

###### Art. 495

The perpetrator of serious non-slanderous insult, committed by word or deed, in writing, images or emblems, in any of the circumstances indicated in Art. 491, shall be punished with imprisonment of three to six months and a fine of six to twelve dollars of the United States of North America; and in the circumstances of Art. 492, with imprisonment of fifteen days to three months and a fine of six dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 146 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

Jurisprudence:

•	Judicial Gazette, PRESS TRIAL, 02-Oct-1969
•	Judicial Gazette, CRIMES COMMITTED BY THE PRESS, 21-Jan-1976

###### Art. 496

When the insults are reciprocal in the same act, none of the offended persons may bring action for those that have been inflicted in said act, whatever the gravity of the non-slanderous insults that have been reciprocally directed; but there is no compensation between slanderous insults and non-slanderous ones.

Jurisprudence:

•	Judicial Gazette, COMPENSATION OF INSULTS NULLIFIES THE CRIME, 19-Apr-2007
•	Judicial Gazette, ACQUITTAL OF DEFENDANTS, 27-Mar-2009

###### Art. 497

The accused of non-slanderous insult shall not be admitted proof of the truth of the imputations.

###### Art. 498

Insults, slanderous or not, published in foreign media outlets, may be prosecuted against the persons who have sent the articles or the order to insert them, or contributed to the introduction or distribution of such media outlets in Ecuador.

Jurisprudence:

•	Judicial Gazette, CRIME OF INSULT THROUGH ELECTRONIC MAIL, 07-May-2007

###### Art. 499

Also responsible for insults, in any of their classes, are the reproducers of articles, images or insulting emblems, without being able to allege in this case, or in that of the previous article, as a cause of justification or excuse that said articles, images or emblems are nothing other than the reproduction of publications made in Ecuador or abroad.

###### Art. 499

A.- Defamation constitutes the dissemination, by any means of social communication or other public character, except that authorized by Law, of the names and surnames of debtors either to require payment from them or using any form that indicates that the named person has that status. Those responsible shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment of six months to two years.

Note: Article added by Law No. 167, published in Official Register 771 of June 22, 1984.

###### Art. 500

Speeches delivered before judges or courts shall not give rise to an action for insult, when the imputations have been made by virtue of the defense of the case; as when challenges are made to the adversary's witnesses and they are proven, to nullify the value of their testimony.

However, the judges may, either ex officio or at the request of a party, order the return of writings containing insults of any kind; warn the lawyers or the parties, and even impose a fine of up to sixteen dollars of the United States of North America, applying for this purpose the rules of the Organic Law of the Judicial Function.
Imputations unrelated to the case give rise to the corresponding action, without prejudice to the fine referred to in the previous paragraph.

Note: Article amended by Art. 147 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 501

Those guilty of any kind of insult who, outside the cases determined in the previous articles, communicating with several persons, even in singular acts, with respect to each of them, offend the reputation, shall be punished as authors of defamation, with a penalty of three months to one year of imprisonment and a fine of six to nineteen dollars of the United States of North America; singular proof being admitted with respect to each of the acts, and provided that these exceed three.

Note: Article amended by Art. 148 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 502

They do not commit insult: parents nor ascendants, with respect to their children and descendants; nor guardians, curators, employers, teachers, directors or heads of educational, correctional or punishment establishments, with respect to their wards, workers, students or dependents, unless the insult is one of those qualified as slanderous.

OF PIMPING AND CORRUPTION OF MINORS

#### CAPITULO I

Of adultery

#### CHAPTER II

Of indecent assault, rape and statutory rape

Art. ..- Whoever subjects a person under eighteen years of age or with a disability, to compel them to perform acts of a sexual nature, without carnal access, shall be punished with ordinary major imprisonment of four to eight years.  
Note: Article added by Law No. 2, published in Official Register 45 of June 23, 2005.  
Note: Interpretation of the unnumbered Art. incorporated by article 9 of the Reformatory Law to the Penal Code that Typifies the Crimes of Sexual Exploitation of

Minors, published in Official Register No. 45, of June 23, 2005, in the sense that: "The constitutive elements of the conducts that were typified until June 22, 2005, in articles 505, 506 and 507 of the Penal Code, which sanctioned acts executed against the sexual integrity of minors, but without carnal access, considered as indecent assault, have not been eliminated, they are subsumed in the article being interpreted, since it has been in force". The words "subjects"; and, "compel them", contained in this article, shall be understood as momentary or permanent acts to break the will of the victim and/or as the performance of actions with which it is intended to obtain or is obtained, through physical violence, threats or any form of inducement or deception directed at a person under eighteen years of age or disabled, to accept or obey and perform acts of a sexual nature, without carnal access, whether on the victim's own body, on the body of a third party or on the body of the active subject.  

Issued by Law No. 53, published in Supplementary Official Register 350 of September 6, 2006.

###### Art. 508.- The assault exists from the moment there is a beginning of execution

###### Art. 509

Statutory rape is defined as copulation with a person, using seduction or deception to obtain their consent.  

Note: Article amended by Law No. 2, published in Official Register 45 of June 23, 2005.

###### Art. 510

Statutory rape shall be punished with imprisonment of three months to three years if the victim is over fourteen years and under eighteen years of age.  

Note: Article amended by Law No. 2, published in Official Register 45 of June 23, 2005.  

Concordances:

•	CODE OF CRIMINAL PROCEDURE 2000, Arts. 36

###### Art. 511

Note: Article repealed by Law No. 2, published in Official Register 45 of June 23, 2005.

###### Art. 511

A.- Whoever requests favors of a sexual nature, for themselves or for a third party, taking advantage of a situation of labor, teaching, religious or similar superiority, with the express or tacit announcement of causing harm to the victim, or to their family, related to the legitimate expectations they may have in the context of said relationship, shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to two years.  

With the same penalty shall be punished whoever, in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph, acts taking advantage of the fact of being in charge of procedures or resolutions of any kind.  

Whoever requests favors or makes malicious insinuations of a sexual nature that violate the sexual integrity of another person, and that is not provided for in the preceding paragraphs, shall be punished with imprisonment of three months to one year.  

The sanctions provided for in this article necessarily include the permanent prohibition from carrying out activities that involve contact with the victim.  

If sexual harassment is committed against minors, it shall be punished

with imprisonment of two to four years.  

Note: Article added by Law No. 105, published in Official Register 365 of July 21, 1998.  
Note: Article replaced by Law No. 2, published in Official Register 45 of June 23, 2005.

###### Art. 512

Rape is carnal access, with total or partial introduction of the male member, by oral, anal or vaginal route; or, the introduction, by vaginal or anal route, of objects, fingers or organs other than the male member, to a person of any sex, in the following cases:

1o.- When the victim is under fourteen years of age;  
2o.- When the offended person is deprived of reason or sense, or when due to illness or any other cause they could not resist; and,
3o.- When violence, threat or intimidation is used.  

Note: Article replaced by Law No. 105, published in Official Register 365 of July 21, 1998.  
Note: Article amended by Law No. 2, published in Official Register 45 of June 23, 2005.

###### Art. 512

A.-Note: Article added by Law No. 105, published in Official Register 365 of July 21, 1998.

###### Art. 513

The crime of rape shall be punished with special major imprisonment of sixteen to twenty-five years, in number 1 of the preceding article; and, with extraordinary major imprisonment of twelve to sixteen years, in numbers 2 and 3 of the same article.  

Note: Article substituted by Supreme Decree 2636, published in Official Register 621 of July 4, 1978.  
Note: Supreme Decree 2636 repealed by Legislative Decree s/n, published in Official Register 36 of October 1, 1979, which orders a return to the previous legal text. Note: Article substituted by Law No. 47, published in Official Register 422 of September 28, 2001.  
Note: Article amended by Law No. 2, published in Official Register 45 of June 23, 2005.  

Concordances:

•	CIVIL CODE (

## BOOK I)

, Arts. 253

###### Art. 514

If the rape produces a serious disturbance in the health of the raped person, the penalty established for numerals 2 and 3 of the preceding article shall be applied; and, if it produces death, the penalty shall be special major imprisonment of sixteen to twenty-five years.  

The same penalty of special major imprisonment of sixteen to twenty-five years shall be imposed on those responsible for rape if the victims are their descendants, ascendants, siblings or relatives by affinity in direct line; and they must, as the case may be, also be sentenced to loss of parental authority.  

Note: Article amended by Supreme Decree 2636, published in Official Register 621 of July 4, 1978.  
Note: Supreme Decree 2636 repealed by Legislative Decree s/n, published in Official Register 36 of October 1, 1979, which orders a return to the previous legal text. Note: Article substituted by Law No. 47, published in Official Register 422 of September 28, 2001.  
Note: Article amended by Law No. 2, published in Official Register 45 of June 23, 2005.  

Concordances:

•	CIVIL CODE (

## BOOK I)

, Arts. 283, 305

###### Art. 515

The minimum of the penalties indicated by the preceding articles shall be increased by four years:

If those responsible are among those who have authority over the victim.  

If they are tutors, or their servants, or servants of the persons designated above;  

If the offense has been committed either by public officials, or ministers of worship, who have abused their position to commit it; or health professionals and personnel responsible for the care and attention of the patient, midwives, or practitioners, on persons entrusted to their care; and,

If in the cases of Arts. 507 and 512, the guilty party, whoever he may be, has been aided in the execution of the crime by one or more persons.  

Note: Article amended by Law No. 105, published in Official Register 365 of July 21, 1998.  
Note: Article amended by Law No. 47, published in Official Register 422 of September 28, 2001.  

Jurisprudence:

•	Judicial Gazette, AGGRAVATE THE PENALTY TO THE ACCUSED FOR KINSHIP WITH THE AGGRIEVED, 27-Jan-1994
•	Judicial Gazette, CRIME OF RAPE, 04-May-1998

###### Art. 516

In cases of homosexualism, which do not constitute rape, the two offenders shall be punished with major imprisonment of four to eight years.  

When homosexualism is committed by the father or other ascendant upon the person of the son or other descendant, the penalty shall be major imprisonment of eight to twelve years and deprivation of the rights and prerogatives that the Civil Code grants over the person and property of the child.  

If it has been committed by ministers of worship, schoolteachers, college professors or tutors, upon persons entrusted to their direction or care, the penalty shall be major imprisonment of eight to twelve years.  

Note: Partially accept the claim filed and declare the unconstitutionality of the first paragraph of article 516 of the Penal Code, and totally suspend the effects of said paragraph, which textually states: "In cases of homosexualism, which do not constitute rape, the two offenders shall be punished with major imprisonment of four to eight years". Given by Constitutional Court Resolution No. 106, published in Supplemental Official Register 203 of November 27, 1997.

###### Art. 517.- Bestiality shall be punished with major imprisonment of four to eight years

#### CHAPTER III

Note: Chapter III with its respective articles from 521 to 528 repealed by Law No. 105, published in Official Register 365 of July 21, 1998.

#### CHAPTER III

ON THE CRIMES OF PROCURING AND CORRUPTION OF MINORS

Art. ...- Whoever promotes or facilitates the prostitution of another person shall be punished with a prison sentence of one to three years, unless he has in his charge a house of tolerance, established in accordance with the regulations that the competent authority may issue for this class of establishments.

Note: Chapter and article added by Law No. 105, published in Official Register 365 of July 21, 1998.
Art. ...- The penalty shall be six to nine years of extraordinary minor imprisonment, without the exemption of the previous article applying, when:

1.	The victim is under fourteen years of age;
2.	Violence, deceit, abuse of authority or any other coercive means is employed;
3.	The victim is for any reason deprived of the capacity to give consent;
4.	The perpetrator is a relative within the fourth degree of consanguinity or second degree of affinity, or is a spouse, cohabitant, adopter, guardian or curator or has under his care for any reason the prostituted person;
5.	The victim is in a situation of abandonment or extreme economic necessity; and,
6.	The perpetrator has made procuring his occupation or way of life.

Note: Article added by Law No. 105, published in Official Register 365 of July 21, 1998.

Art. ...- A prison sentence of two to four years shall be imposed on whoever exploits the earnings obtained by a person who engages in prostitution.

If the victim is under fourteen years of age, or a descendant, adopted child, child of his spouse or cohabitant or is under his care, the penalty shall be three to six years of ordinary minor imprisonment.

Note: Article added by Law No. 105, published in Official Register 365 of July 21, 1998.

Art. ...- The conduct of whoever by seduction or deceit abducts a person to deliver him to another for the purpose of having sexual relations shall be deemed procuring.

Note: Article added by Law No. 105, published in Official Register 365 of July 21, 1998.

Art. ...- Whoever promotes or facilitates the entry or exit from the country or the transfer within the territory of the Republic of persons to engage in prostitution shall be punished with a sentence of two to four years.

If any of the aggravating circumstances provided for in the previous articles is present, the penalty shall be three to six years of ordinary minor imprisonment.

Note: Article added by Law No. 105, published in Official Register 365 of July 21, 1998.

Art. ...- A prison sentence of one to three years shall be imposed for:
1.	The exhibition, sale or delivery to minors under fourteen years of age of obscene objects, books, writings, visual or auditory images, that may seriously affect modesty or excite or pervert their sexual instinct; and,
2.	Whoever incites a minor under fourteen years of age to drunkenness or the practice of obscene acts or facilitates his entry to brothels or other centers of corruption such as cinemas or theaters that offer obscene shows.

Note: Article added by Law No. 105, published in Official Register 365 of July 21, 1998.

CHAPTER ...
ON THE CRIMES OF SEXUAL EXPLOITATION

Art. ..- Whoever produces, publishes or commercializes pornographic images, visual, audiovisual, computer, electronic materials or any other physical support or format, or organizes live shows, with pornographic scenes in which persons over fourteen and under eighteen years of age participate, shall be punished with a sentence of six to nine years of ordinary minor imprisonment, the confiscation of the objects and property that are the product of the crime, and disqualification from employment, profession or occupation.

The same penalty shall be incurred by whoever distributes pornographic images, whose external characteristics make it manifest that the exhibition of persons over twelve and under eighteen years of age at the time of the creation of the image is recorded or photographed in them.

The same penalty shall be imposed on whoever facilitates access to pornographic shows or supplies pornographic material in whose images minors participate.

When in these offenses, the victim is a minor under twelve years of age or disabled, or a person suffering from a serious incurable disease, the penalty shall be extraordinary major imprisonment of twelve to sixteen years, payment of compensation, confiscation of the objects and property that are the product of the crime, disqualification from employment, profession or occupation; and, in case of recidivism, the penalty shall be twenty-five years of special major imprisonment.

When the offender of these crimes is the father, the mother, relatives up to the fourth degree of consanguinity and second degree of affinity, guardians, legal representatives, curators or any person from the intimate circle of the family, ministers of worship, teachers and professors and, any other person who by their profession or occupation has abused the victim, they shall be punished with a sentence of sixteen to twenty-five years of extraordinary major imprisonment, payment of compensation, confiscation of the objects and property that are the product of the crime, disqualification from employment, profession or occupation.

If the victim is under twelve years of age, the maximum penalty shall be applied.
Note: Chapter and Articles added by Law No. 2, published in Official Register 45 of June 23, 2005.
Art. ..- Whoever with violence, threat, intimidation or deceit uses persons of legal age, in shows that involve the total or partial exhibition of their body for sexual purposes, shall be punished with ordinary major imprisonment of four to eight years.

Note: Article added by Law No. 2, published in Official Register 45 of June 23, 2005.

Art. ..- Whoever organizes, offers or promotes tourist activities that involve services of a sexual nature, shall be punished with ordinary minor imprisonment of six to nine years and a fine of ten thousand to fifteen thousand dollars of the United States of America and the extinction of the legal entity or the closure of the company, if it belongs to a natural person.

The penalty shall be extraordinary minor imprisonment of nine to twelve years, if the victim is a person under eighteen years of age and a fine of fifteen thousand to twenty thousand dollars of the United States of America.

The penalty of extraordinary major imprisonment of nine to twelve years shall be applied, in the following cases:

1.	When the victim is under twelve years of age;
2.	When the offender takes advantage of the vulnerability of the victim or the victim is incapacitated to resist, or violence, threat or intimidation is used;
3.	When the offender is a spouse, cohabitant or relative up to the fourth degree of consanguinity or second degree of affinity of the victim; and,
4.	If the offender has any type of relationship of trust or authority, or is a legal representative, stepfather or stepmother of the victim or minister of worship.

Note: Article added by Law No. 2, published in Official Register 45 of June 23, 2005.

Art. ..- Whoever, by any means, acquires or contracts tourist activities, knowing that they involve services of a sexual nature with persons under eighteen years of age, shall be punished with ordinary major imprisonment of eight to twelve years.

Note: Article added by Law No. 2, published in Official Register 45 of June 23, 2005.

Art. ..- Whoever promotes, induces, participates, facilitates or favors the recruitment, transportation, harboring, reception or delivery of persons by resorting to threat, violence, deceit or any other fraudulent form for purposes of sexual exploitation, shall be punished with ordinary major imprisonment of eight to twelve years. If the victim is a person under eighteen years of age, the maximum penalty shall be applied.
The penalty of extraordinary major imprisonment of twelve to sixteen years shall be applied, when one or more of the following circumstances concur:

1.	If the victim is a person under twelve years of age;
2.	If there is abuse of authority, or of a situation of necessity or vulnerability of the victim;
3.	If the offender is a spouse, cohabitant or relative up to the fourth degree of consanguinity or second degree of affinity of the victim;
4.	If the offender has any type of relationship of trust, authority, is a legal representative, stepfather or stepmother of the victim or minister of worship; and,
5.	If the victim, as a consequence of the crime, suffers a physical injury or permanent psychological damage or contracts a serious or fatal disease.

Note: Article added by Law No. 2, published in Official Register 45 of June 23, 2005.

Art. ..- In the event that by the commission of any of the crimes of this Chapter, the

death of the victim occurs, the penalty shall be special major imprisonment of sixteen to twenty-five years.

Note: Article added by Law No. 2, published in Official Register 45 of June 23, 2005.

Art. ..- Whoever induces, promotes, favors, facilitates the sexual exploitation of persons under eighteen years of age, or of those who have disabilities, in exchange for remuneration or any other retribution, or appropriates all or part of these values, shall be punished with a sentence of ordinary minor imprisonment of six to nine years and the confiscation of property acquired with the proceeds of the crime and payment of compensation for damages.

If the victim is under fourteen years of age, the penalty shall be extraordinary major imprisonment of twelve to sixteen years. In case of recidivism, the penalty shall be special major imprisonment of sixteen to twenty-five years.

Note: Article added by Law No. 2, published in Official Register 45 of June 23, 2005.

CHAPTER ...
COMMON PROVISIONS ON SEXUAL CRIMES AND TRAFFICKING IN PERSONS

Art. ..- In the case of concurrence of sexual crimes and/or trafficking in persons, the penalties shall be accumulated up to a maximum of thirty-five years.
Note: Chapter and Article added by Law No. 2, published in Official Register 45 of June 23, 2005.

Art. ..- In the crimes contemplated in Title VIII, of Book II of the Penal Code, the criminal action shall prescribe in double the time of the maximum penalty provided for each offense, without the term being able to exceed fifty years. The penalty shall prescribe in a time equal to double the sentence, but the prescription period shall never be greater than thirty-five years nor less than five years.

Note: Article added by Law No. 2, published in Official Register 45 of June 23, 2005.

Art. ..- The public non-private behavior of the victim, prior to the commission of the sexual crime or trafficking in persons, shall not be considered within the process.

Note: Article added by Law No. 2, published in Official Register 45 of June 23, 2005.

Art. ..- In sexual crimes, the consent given by the victim under eighteen

years of age, shall be irrelevant. In crimes of trafficking in persons, consent shall be irrelevant.

Note: Article added by Law No. 2, published in Official Register 45 of June 23, 2005.

Art. ..- If the author or those responsible for the commission of sexual crimes or trafficking in persons, at the time of committing the offense, exercises with respect to the victim his parental authority or legal representation, he shall be punished, in addition to the corresponding penalty, with the indefinite loss of these.

Note: Article added by Law No. 2, published in Official Register 45 of June 23, 2005.

Art. ..- When the media make an apology for sexual crimes and trafficking in persons, the respective legal representatives shall be punished with fines of up to twenty thousand dollars of the United States of America, without prejudice to the confiscation of the products or means used for their commission.

In case of recidivism, the closure and reversion of frequencies or authorization for their operation shall proceed.

Note: Article added by Law No. 2, published in Official Register 45 of June 23, 2005.

Art. ..- Matters related to conditional release, reduction of penalties, modification of the penalty, suspension of the execution of the penalty, conditional sentence and conditional release, provided for in Book I, Title IV, Chapter II of the Penal Code, shall not be considered or applied in the case of crimes contemplated in Title VIII of Book II of the Penal Code, when they have been committed against persons under eighteen years of age.
Note: Article added by Law No. 2, published in Official Register 45 of June 23, 2005.

Art. ..- Whoever alters the identity of a child; substitutes him for another; simulates a pregnancy or birth; delivers or records false or supposed data about a birth; usurps the legitimate paternity or maternity of a child; or, falsely declares the death of a newborn, shall be punished with extraordinary major imprisonment of twelve to sixteen years and a fine of fifteen thousand to twenty thousand dollars of the United States of America.".

Note: Article added by Law No. 2, published in Official Register 45 of June 23, 2005.


This excerpt sits under: PENAL CODE > BOOK I)

#### CHAPTER IV

Of abduction

###### Art. 529

Whoever, with dishonest purposes, by means of violence, artifice or threats, shall have abducted or caused to be abducted a minor over seven years of age, shall be punished with imprisonment of one to five years and a fine of six to sixteen dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 149 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

Case law:
•	Judicial Gazette, ABDUCTION, 03-Mar-1888
•	Judicial Gazette, CRIME OF ABDUCTION, 20-Mar-1906
•	Judicial Gazette, CRIME OF ABDUCTION, OF STATUTORY RAPE, OF SEDUCTION, 29-Sep-1967

###### Art. 530

If the abducted person is a girl under sixteen years of age, the penalty shall be three to six years of minor imprisonment.

Case law:
•	Judicial Gazette, CRIME OF ABDUCTION, 23-Jul-1982

###### Art. 531

Whoever shall have abducted or caused to be abducted a woman over sixteen years of age and under eighteen, who shall have consented to her abduction and voluntarily followed the abductor, shall be punished with one to five years of imprisonment.

Concordances:

•	CODE OF CRIMINAL PROCEDURE 2000, Arts. 36, 40

Case law:

•	Judicial Gazette, CRIMES OF ABDUCTION AND STATUTORY RAPE, 02-Jun-1994

###### Art. 532

The abductor who marries the minor whom he shall have abducted or caused to be abducted, and those who shall have taken part in the abduction, may not be prosecuted except after the marriage has been definitively declared null.

Concordances:
•	CIVIL CODE (

## BOOK I)

, Arts. 96, 253

### TITLE IX

OF CRIMES AGAINST CIVIL STATUS

#### CHAPTER I

Of the celebration of illegal marriages

###### Art. 533

Whoever contracts a second or subsequent marriage, knowing that the previous one was not legally dissolved, shall be punished with two to five years of imprisonment.

Concordances:
•	CIVIL CODE (

## BOOK I)

, Arts. 92, 105, 128
•	CODE OF PRIVATE INTERNATIONAL LAW SANCHEZ DE BUSTAMANTE, Arts. 52

###### Art. 534

Whoever, in the celebration of marriages, does not comply with the laws established in the Republic, shall be punished with imprisonment of one to five years.

Concordances:
•	CIVIL CODE (

## BOOK I)

, Arts. 81, 89, 100, 101, 102

###### Art. 535

Whoever employs fraud or violence to evade the laws in force that regulate the celebration or termination of marriage, shall be punished with three to five years of imprisonment.

Concordances:

•	CIVIL CODE (

## BOOK I)

, Arts. 105

###### Art. 536

Whoever, in an illegal but valid marriage, causes the authority to intervene by surprise or deceit, shall be punished with six months to two years of imprisonment; and,

If he causes it to intervene with violence or intimidation, he shall be punished with minor imprisonment of three to six years.
If someone impersonates an authority for the celebration of a marriage, he shall be punished with extraordinary minor imprisonment of nine to twelve years.
The same penalty shall be imposed on the contracting party who has caused said pretended official to intervene.

###### Art. 537

The guardian or curator who, before the legal approval of his accounts, contracts

marriage, or gives his consent for his children or descendants to contract it with the person he has or had under guardianship, shall be punished with one to five years of imprisonment and a fine of sixteen to one hundred fifty-six dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 150 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

Concordances:
•	CIVIL CODE (

## BOOK I)

, Arts. 90

###### Art. 538

The authority who celebrates a marriage for which there is a non-dispensable impediment, shall be punished with a fine of eight to seventy-seven dollars of the United States of North America and imprisonment of one to five years.
If the impediment is dispensable, the penalty shall be reduced by half.
Note: Article amended by Art. 151 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

Concordances:

•	CIVIL CODE (

## BOOK I)

, Arts. 82, 89, 95, 96

###### Art. 539

In all cases of this Chapter, the fraudulent contracting party shall be sentenced to provide a dowry, according to his means, to the woman who has contracted marriage in good faith.

Concordances:

•	CIVIL CODE (

## BOOK I)

, Arts. 94

###### Art. 540

The authority who issues dispensations and authorizations for the celebration of a marriage, without prior presentation of the written consent of the parents, or guardians of the minor contracting parties, or of the judge, as the case may be, shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to one year and a fine of sixty-two to one hundred fifty-six dollars of the United States of North America.
Note: Article amended by Art. 152 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

Concordances:

•	CIVIL CODE (

## BOOK I)

, Arts. 82, 89

#### CHAPTER II

Of crimes directed at destroying or preventing proof of the civil status of a child

###### Art. 541

Whoever having found a newborn child, has not delivered him within three days, to the political lieutenant, or police authority of the place where he was found, shall be punished with imprisonment of eight days to three months.

###### Art. 542

Those guilty of substitution of one child for another; or of simulation of a birth; or of usurpation of the civil status of a person, shall be punished with minor imprisonment of three to six years.

Concordances:

•	CIVIL CODE (

## BOOK I)

, Arts. 244, 261

Case law:

•	Judicial Gazette, CRIME OF KIDNAPPING OF A MINOR, 23-Jul-1993

###### Art. 543

Whoever shall have abducted or caused to be abducted a child, and provided that the crime does not constitute a kidnapping, shall be punished with minor imprisonment of three to six years, even if the child has voluntarily followed the guilty party.

Note: Included Errata, published in Official Register 165 of February 17, 1971
, and published again in Official Register 173 of March 3, 1971.

###### Art. 544

Whoever maliciously shall have concealed or caused to be concealed a child, if the act is not more severely punished in this Code, shall be punished with imprisonment of one to five years and a fine of six to twelve dollars of the United States of North America.
Note: Article amended by Art. 153 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 545

Those who shall have taken or caused to be taken to a foundling home or other establishments intended for that purpose, a child who was entrusted to them, shall be punished with imprisonment of one to three months and a fine of six to nine dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 154 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

Concordances:

•	CIVIL CODE (

## BOOK I)

, Arts. 269

###### Art. 546

Those who, being in charge of a child, do not make it known to the persons who have the right to claim him, shall be punished with imprisonment of eight days to one year and a fine of six to twelve dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 155 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

Concordances:

•	GENERAL LAW OF CIVIL REGISTRY IDENTIFICATION AND IDENTITY CARDS, Arts. 62

### TITLE X

OF CRIMES AGAINST PROPERTY

#### CHAPTER I

Of theft

###### Art. 547

Those who, without violence or threats against persons, nor force against things, fraudulently take another's property, with intent to appropriate it, are guilty of theft.

In addition, individuals of recognized criminal conduct who habitually engage in the commission of crimes against property and who are registered as such in the State Security Offices are considered guilty of theft. The penalty for this class of offenders shall be one to three years of imprisonment.  
Note: The Constitutional Guarantees Tribunal resolved to totally suspend due to substantive unconstitutionality the second paragraph of this article, whose text precedes. Provision given by Resolution of the Constitutional Guarantees Tribunal, published in Official Register 632 of February 24, 1987.  
Congress by Resolution published in Official Register 677 of May 4, 1987 ratifies the Resolution of the Constitutional Guarantees Tribunal.

, Arts. 583, 584
•	CIVIL CODE (

###### Art. 548

Theft shall be punished with imprisonment from one month to three years, taking into account the value of the stolen things.

###### Art. 549.- The penalty shall be six months to five years of imprisonment

1o.- When it involves machines or work instruments left in the field, or wires or other elements of fences, causing their total or partial destruction; 2o.- When the theft is committed on the occasion of a fire, explosion, flood, shipwreck, railway accident, riot or mutiny, or taking advantage of the facilities arising from any other disaster or public commotion;  
3o.- When it involves tools, farming implements or other utensils, or animals that the victim needs for the exercise of his profession, art, trade or work; and,
4o.- When the persons from whom things are stolen are miserable or needy, or when what is stolen from them is sufficient to ruin their property.

#### CHAPTER II

Of robbery

###### Art. 550

Whoever, by means of violence or threats against persons or force against things, fraudulently takes another's property, with intent to appropriate it, is guilty of robbery, whether the violence takes place before the act to facilitate it, at the moment of committing it, or after it has been committed to procure impunity.

, Arts. 1633
•	CODE OF CRIMINAL PROCEDURE 2000, Arts. 106

###### Art. 551

Robbery shall be punished with imprisonment from one to five years and with minor confinement from three to six years in cases where it is perpetrated with violence against persons, taking into consideration the value of the stolen things.  

Note: Article amended by Law No. 47, published in Official Register 422 of September 28, 2001.

###### Art. 552

The maximum penalty established in the preceding article shall be applied to the person responsible if any of the following circumstances concur:

1a.- If the violence has produced wounds that do not leave permanent injury;  
2a.- If the robbery has been executed with weapons, or at night, or in an uninhabited place, or in a gang, or on roads or public thoroughfares;  
3a.- If the robbery is perpetrated with perforation or fracture of a wall, enclosure, roof or floor, door or window of an inhabited place or its immediate dependencies; and,
4a.- When any of the circumstances of numbers 2o, 3o and 4o of Art. 549 concur.  

When two or more of the circumstances referred to in this article concur, the penalty shall be minor confinement from six to nine years.  

If the violence has caused a permanent injury of those detailed in Arts. 466 and 467, the penalty shall be major confinement from eight to twelve years.  

If the violence has caused death, the penalty shall be special major confinement from sixteen to thirty years.  

Note: Article amended by Supreme Decree 2636, published in Official Register 621 of July 4, 1978.  
Note: Supreme Decree 2636 repealed by Legislative Decree s/n, published in Official Register 36 of October 1, 1979, which orders return to the previous legal text. Note: Article amended by Law No. 47, published in Official Register 422 of September 28, 2001.

Art. ...- Whoever, having dispossessed the driver or occupants of a motor vehicle, uses it for the purpose of committing other crimes, even if there is no intent to appropriate the vehicle, shall be punished with ordinary minor confinement from three to six years.  

Note: Article added by Law No. 21, published in Official Register 154 of November 28, 2005.  

Art. ...- If as a consequence of the commission of these crimes serious physical injuries or psychological damage are produced in the victim or victims, the perpetrator or perpetrators shall be sanctioned with extraordinary major confinement from eight to twelve years.  

Note: Article added by Law No. 21, published in Official Register 154 of November 28, 2005.  

Art. ...- Those who in the commission of the crimes typified in this Law have caused the death or permanent incapacity of the victim or victims shall be punished with special major confinement from 16 to 25 years.  

Note: Article added by Law No. 21, published in Official Register 154 of November 28, 2005.

###### Art. 553

The following is assimilated to robbery: the taking of another's property done with fraud and intent to appropriate, even if there are no acts of violence or threats against persons nor force against things, if it has been carried out on trains, streetcars, buses, docks, public gatherings or other crowds.

Also punishable with the penalty indicated in Art. 552 is the taking of objects, implements, materials or things belonging to installations intended for the service of Fire Departments and the fraudulent purchase of such objects, materials or things.

Jurisprudencia:

•	Gaceta Judicial, DELITO DE FRAUDE, 21-ene-1947

Art. ...- Apropiación ilícita.- Those who fraudulently use information systems or electronic networks to facilitate the appropriation of another's property, or those who procure the non-consensual transfer of property, securities or rights of a person, to the detriment of that person or a third party, for their own benefit or that of another person by altering, manipulating or modifying the operation of electronic networks, computer programs, computer systems, telematics systems or data messages, shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of five hundred to one thousand United States dollars.

Nota: Artículo agregado por Ley No. 67, publicada en Registro Oficial Suplemento 557 de 17 de Abril del 2002 .

Art. ...- The penalty of imprisonment of one to five years and a fine of one thousand to two thousand United States dollars, if the crime has been committed using the following means:

1.	Disabling of alarm or security systems;
2.	Discovery or decryption of secret or encrypted keys;
3.	Use of magnetic or perforated cards;
4.	Use of remote opening controls or instruments; and,
5.	Violation of electronic, computer or other similar security measures.

Nota: Artículo agregado por Ley No. 67, publicada en Registro Oficial Suplemento 557 de 17 de Abril del 2002 .

#### CAPITULO III

Del abigeato

###### Art. 554

The theft or robbery of horse, cattle, swine or sheep livestock, committed in places intended for the preservation, breeding or fattening thereof, constitutes the crime of cattle rustling, without regard to the value of the livestock taken.

Concordancias:

•	CODIGO DE PROCEDIMIENTO PENAL 2000, Arts. 106

Jurisprudencia:

•	Gaceta Judicial, ABIGEATO, 11-may-1921
•	Gaceta Judicial, ABIGEATO, 18-ene-1939
•	Gaceta Judicial, ABIGEATO, 22-feb-1960

###### Art. 555

Cattle rustling shall be punished with a penalty of one to three years of imprisonment, in case of theft; and two to five years of imprisonment, in case of robbery.

Recidivism shall be punished with double such penalties.

###### Art. 556

If cattle rustling committed with violence has caused wounds or injuries, or the death of any person, the penalties established for such cases in the Chapter on robbery shall be applied to the guilty party.

#### CAPITULO IV

De la extorsión

###### Art. 557

Anyone who, by intimidation, or by simulating public authority, or a false order thereof, compels another, without depriving him of personal liberty, to deliver, send, deposit, or place at his disposal or that of a third party things, money or documents that produce or may produce legal effects, shall be punished with imprisonment of one to five years.

###### Art. 558

Anyone who, by the same means, or with violence, compels another, without depriving him of personal liberty, to sign or destroy documents of obligation or credit, shall incur the same penalty established in the preceding article.

###### Art. 559

Anyone who, with threats of imputations against honor, or of violation of secrets, or of publications that affect honor or reputation, commits any of the acts set forth in the two preceding articles, shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to four years.

#### CAPITULO V

De las estafas y otras defraudaciones

###### Art. 560

Anyone who has fraudulently diverted or dissipated to the detriment of another, effects, money, merchandise, bills, receipts, writings of any kind, containing obligation or discharge, and which have been delivered to him with the condition of returning them, or making a specific use or employment thereof, shall be punished with imprisonment of one to five years and a fine of eight to sixteen United States dollars.

Nota: Artículo reformado por Art. 156 de Ley No. 75, publicada en Registro Oficial 635 de 7 de Agosto del 2002 .

###### Art. 561

Anyone who has abused the needs, weaknesses or passions of a minor, to make him sign, to his detriment, obligations, receipts, discharges, drafts, or any other obligatory documents, whatever the form in which this transaction has been made or disguised, shall be punished with imprisonment of three months to five years and a fine of six to twelve United States dollars.

Nota: Artículo reformado por Art. 157 de Ley No. 75, publicada en Registro Oficial 635 de 7 de Agosto del 2002 .

###### Art. 562

Anyone who, after having produced in court any title, document or memorial, has fraudulently removed it, in whatever manner, shall be punished with a fine of six to thirty-one United States dollars.

This penalty shall be applied summarily by the court or judge hearing the case.

Nota: Artículo reformado por Art. 158 de Ley No. 75, publicada en Registro Oficial 635 de 7 de Agosto del 2002 .

###### Art. 563

Whoever, with the purpose of appropriating a thing belonging to another, has had funds, movable property, obligations, receipts, or acquittances delivered to him, either by using false names or false qualities, or by employing fraudulent maneuvers to make believe in the existence of false enterprises, of a power, or of an imaginary credit, to instill hope or fear of an event, accident, or any other

chimerical occurrence, or to otherwise abuse confidence or credulity, shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of eight to one hundred fifty-six dollars of the United States of North America.

Whoever commits the crime using electronic or telematic means shall be sanctioned with the maximum penalty provided in the preceding paragraph and a fine of five hundred to one thousand dollars of the United States of North America.

The penalty shall be ordinary minor imprisonment of three to six years, if the fraud is committed in cases of illegal migrations.

Note: Article amended by Law No. 67, published in Official Registry Supplement 557 of April 17, 2002.
Note: Article amended by Art. 159 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.
Note: Last paragraph added by Law No. 91, published in Official Registry 716 of December 2, 2002.
