###### Art. 563

A.- It corresponds exclusively to the Ecuadorian Housing Bank, to the Mutual Housing Savings and Loan Associations, and to the Housing Cooperatives with legal personality, whose registration has been recorded in the National Housing Board, to receive funds from natural persons, as fees or contributions for urbanization or social housing construction programs.

Those who receive or order the receipt of fees, contributions or advances, in contravention of the provisions of the preceding paragraph, shall incur in the crime of fraud, which shall be prosecuted ex officio, and shall be sanctioned with the penalty established in the preceding article.

Popular action is established to denounce the crime typified in the preceding paragraph.

Note: Inorganic amendment given by Art. 2 of Supreme Decree No. 1206, published in Official Registry 419 of October 26, 1973.

###### Art. 564

Whoever has deceived the buyer shall be punished with imprisonment of one month to one year and a fine of six to forty-seven dollars of the United States of North America, or with only one of these penalties:

Regarding the identity of the thing sold, fraudulently delivering a thing different from the determined object on which the contract has dealt; and,

Regarding the nature or origin of the thing sold, delivering a thing similar in appearance to that which has been purchased or believed to have been purchased.

Note: Article amended by Art. 160 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

Concordances:

•	CIVIL CODE (

## BOOK IV)

, Arts. 1732, 1740

###### Art. 565

Those who, with fraudulent maneuvers, have deceived the buyer regarding the quantity of things sold shall be punished with imprisonment of one month to one year and a fine of six to twenty-five dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 161 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 566

The following shall be punished with imprisonment of fifteen days to three months and a fine of six to sixteen dollars of the United States of North America:

Those who, without being in the case of Art. 428, because there is no danger of altering the health of consumers, have falsified or caused to be falsified beverages or foodstuffs;

Those who have sold or caused to be sold, publicly or privately, said falsified articles; and,

Those who, by posters or notices, printed or not, or by any other mode of propaganda, have taught or revealed procedures for the falsification of the mentioned

articles.

Note: Article amended by Art. 162 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 567

Importers, commission agents, or receivers of falsified beverages or foodstuffs shall be punished with the same penalty and a fine of sixteen to sixty-two dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 163 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 568

In dealing with the infractions determined in the preceding articles and in Arts. 428, 429 and 430, the judge shall order the publication of the sentence, by posters and by the press, at the expense of the convicted person; and shall order the closure of the factories, shops, warehouses and stores where the falsified articles are kept or sold, until the issuance of the sentence, and shall order the confiscation to which there may be grounds.

###### Art. 569

Whoever conceals, keeps custody of, guards, transports, sells or transfers possession, in whole or in part, of goods, things or livestock, product of robbery or theft, "or whose legal origin cannot be proven" shall be punished with ordinary minor imprisonment of three to six years and a fine of six to sixteen dollars of the United States of America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 164 of Law No. 75, published in Official Registry 635 of August 7, 2002.
Note: Article replaced by Law No. 0, published in Official Registry Supplement 160 of March 29, 2010.
Note: The phrase in quotation marks declared unconstitutional by Resolution of the Constitutional Court No. 29, published in Official Registry Supplement 343 of December 17, 2010.
Note: Again declares the unconstitutionality on the merits of article 569 of the Penal Code, in the phrase "... whose legal origin cannot be proven"; therefore, article 569 ibid shall be read as follows: "Art. 569.- "Whoever conceals, keeps custody of, guards, transports, sells or transfers possession, in whole or in part, of goods, things or livestock, product of robbery or theft shall be punished with ordinary minor imprisonment of three to six years and a fine of six to sixteen dollars of the United States of America". Given by Resolution of the Constitutional Court No. 33, published in Official Registry Supplement 407 of March 18, 2011.

Jurisprudence:

•	Judicial Gazette, COMMERCE WITH A MINOR, 25-Jan-1995

###### Art. 570

In case of attachment, if the debtor or any other person has fraudulently destroyed or disposed of any of the objects on which the seizure has been made, he shall be

punished with imprisonment of eight days to two years.

Jurisprudence:

•	Judicial Gazette, ARBITRARY DISPOSITION BY THE DEPOSITARY, 19-Dec-1962

###### Art. 571

The following shall be punished with imprisonment of eight days to two years and a fine of six to thirty-one dollars of the United States of North America:

Those who, having found a movable thing belonging to another, whose value exceeds one hundred sucres, or having obtained its possession by chance, shall have concealed it or delivered it to a third party, fraudulently; and,
Those who, having discovered a treasure, shall have appropriated it to the detriment of those who, according to the Law, have a right to the treasure.  
Note: Article amended by Art. 165 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 572

In the case of the second paragraph of the preceding article, if the value of the object that has not been returned to its owner does not exceed one hundred sucres, the concealer or holder shall be punished with a penalty of misdemeanor only.

###### Art. 573

The person who shall have fraudulently procured funds, securities or receipts, by means of a draft drawn against a person who does not exist, or who was not his debtor, or who was not to be so at the time of maturity, or who had not authorized him to draw against him, shall be punished with imprisonment of one to five years and a fine of six to sixty-two dollars of the United States of North America.  

However, prosecution may not take place, or shall cease, if the draft has been paid, or if the drawer shall have paid the amount himself, upon discovery of the fraud.  

Note: Article amended by Art. 166 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 574

The debtor who improperly removes or permits the removal from the place where the industrial or agricultural exploitation is carried out of the objects given as industrial or agricultural pledge, or who by his negligence causes the disappearance or deterioration thereof, exchanges them, abandons them or gives as guarantee as his own agricultural or industrial goods that do not belong to him, shall be punished with imprisonment of one to five years and a fine of nine to four hundred thirty-seven dollars of the United States of North America.  

The same penalty shall be incurred by the debtor of a special commercial pledge or agricultural or industrial pledge who sells, donates or pledges to another person the object constituted as pledge without the intervention of the creditor, or who does not comply within the

term set by the judge with the exhibition or delivery for auction sale of the object given as pledge, changes its place of conservation designated in the contract causing damage to the other party, or maliciously destroys, damages or impairs the integrity thereof without settling the credit.  

The same sanction shall be imposed on the debtor who sells the fruits of the objects pledged as industrial or agricultural pledge or the objects themselves, without giving notice to the buyer of the existence of the pledge contract.  

These proceedings shall be initiated by order of the civil judge, who shall remit all proceedings to the Criminal Judge for the corresponding prosecution of the criminal process. If the pledge is placed at the disposal of the judge up to the rendering of the indictment, the criminal proceeding shall be terminated and it shall be returned to the Civil Judge with the background that was sent, for the continuation of the auction.  

Note: Article amended by Art. 167 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.  

Concordances:

•	CODIGO CIVIL (

## LIBRO II)

, Arts. 696
•	CODIGO DE COMERCIO, Arts. 576, 592

###### Art. 575

The person who shall have purchased movable goods with reservation of title and enters into contracts of sale, exchange, lease or pledge over them, takes them out of the country or delivers them to other persons without having paid the totality of the price, except in the case of express and written authorization from the seller, shall be punished with imprisonment of two months to three years.  
The same penalty shall be applied to the buyer who maliciously causes the things acquired with reservation of title to disappear, who deteriorates or destroys them, who alters the marks, numbers, signs or who by any means prevents their identification.  

In both cases, the provisions of paragraph 4 of the preceding article shall apply.  
Those who fraudulently present and subscribe false declarations and documentation aimed at obtaining illicit exchange or monetary benefits, with over-invoicing or under-invoicing in foreign trade, shall be punished with imprisonment of one to five years, without prejudice to the sanctions established in other legal provisions.  

Those who maliciously perform acts by which they obtain undue exchange or monetary benefits, whether through false declarations or otherwise, shall be punished with imprisonment of three months to four years and a fine of not less than 50% nor more than 200% of the amounts unduly obtained.  

Note: Article amended by article 37 of the Law of Economic Regulation and Control of Public Expenditure, No. 122, published in Supplementary Official Register 453 of March 17, 1983.  
Note: Article amended by Emergency Decree Law No. 2, published in Supplementary Official Register 930 of May 7, 1992, Art.

###### 170. Art. 575

A.- Those who, with the purpose of obtaining personal profit and as leaders, organize pseudo-cooperatives and invade lands both in the urban and rural areas, thereby violating the right of private property, shall be punished with imprisonment of two to five years.  

Note: Article added by Supreme Decree No. 2969, published in Official Register 714 of November 20, 1978.  

Concordances:
•	CONSTITUCION DE LA REPUBLICA DEL ECUADOR, Arts. 282, 321

###### Art. 575

B.- Whoever, claiming the status of member of a pseudo-cooperative, invades lands located in the rural or urban area and negotiates over those or over supposed rights acquired in said lands, shall be punished with imprisonment of one to three years.  

Note: Article added by Supreme Decree No. 2969, published in Official Register 714 of November 20, 1978.

###### Art. 575-C

Note: Article added by article 82 of Law No. 6, published in Official Register 97 of December 29, 1988.  
Note: Article 82 of Law No. 6 replaced by Law No. 51, published in Official Register 349 of December 31, 1993.  
Note: Article repealed by Third Repealing Provision of Legislative Decree No. 0, published in Supplementary Official Register 242 of December 29, 2007.

###### Art. 575

D.- The person who fraudulently, by any act, does not pay the minimum support price for the box of bananas for export, set by current interministerial agreement, as well as the intellectual authors, accomplices and concealers, shall be punished with imprisonment of one to three years and the fine established in article 4 of the Law to Stimulate and Control the Production and Marketing of Bananas.  

Once the criminal proceeding for the infraction referred to in the preceding paragraph has been initiated, the judge shall order as a precautionary measure the suspension of the exporter's trademark and/or patent.  

Note: Article added by Law No. 48, published in Official Register 347 of December 27, 1999.

#### CAPITULO VI

Of bankrupts and other punishable debtors

###### Art. 576

Merchants who, in the cases provided by the laws, are declared guilty or responsible for bankruptcy, shall be punished:

Those of culpable bankruptcy, with imprisonment of one to three years; and,
Those of absconding or fraudulent bankruptcy, with minor imprisonment of three to six years.  

Concordances:
•	CODIGO CIVIL (TITULO PRELIMINAR), Arts. 29
•	CODIGO CIVIL (

## LIBRO IV)

, Arts. 1633
•	CODIGO DE PROCEDIMIENTO CIVIL, Arts. 507, 508, 519, 589
•	CODIGO DE COMERCIO, Arts. 85, 116
•	LEY DE MERCADO DE VALORES, Arts. 216
•	LEY ORGANICA DE INSTITUCIONES DEL SISTEMA FINANCIERO, Arts. 132

###### Art. 577

In the case of bankruptcy of a company or a legal entity engaged in commerce, any director, administrator or manager of the bankrupt company or legal entity, or accountant or bookkeeper, who has cooperated in the execution of any of the culpable or fraudulent acts that determine the bankruptcy, shall be punished with the penalty of fraudulent or culpable bankrupt, as the case may be.

###### Art. 578

When it is not a case of bankruptcy of a merchant, the guilty party shall be punished with imprisonment of one to five years, in the case of fraudulent insolvency, and with imprisonment of six months to two years, in the case of culpable insolvency.

###### Art. 579

The following shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to two years and a fine of six to sixty-two dollars of the United States of North America.

Those who, in favor of the bankrupt, have removed, concealed or hidden, in whole or in part, his movable and immovable property;

Those who have fraudulently presented themselves in the bankruptcy, and maintained, whether in their own name or through the interposition of a person, fictitious or exaggerated credits;

The creditor who has stipulated, whether with the bankrupt or any other person, particular advantages, by reason of his votes in the deliberation relating to the bankruptcy, or who has made a particular contract from which an advantage results in his favor and against the assets of the bankrupt; and,

The trustee of the bankruptcy guilty of embezzlement in the performance of his duties.

Note: Article amended by Art. 168 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

#### CAPITULO VII

Of usurpation

###### Art. 580.- The following shall be punished with imprisonment of one month to two years

1o.- Whoever by violence, deceit or abuse of trust dispossesses another of the possession or tenure of immovable property, or of a real right of use, usufruct, habitation, easement or antichresis, constituted over an immovable;
2o.- Whoever, in order to seize all or part of an immovable, destroys or alters the boundaries or limits thereof; and,
3o.- Whoever, with violence or threats, obstructs the possession of an immovable.

Concordancias:

, Arts. 715, 725, 727, 729, 778, 825, 859

•	Gaceta Judicial, USURPACION, 05-feb-2009
•	Gaceta Judicial, VIOLENCIA, ENGAÑO, 18-feb-2009

###### Art. 581.- The following shall be punished with imprisonment of fifteen days to one year

1o.- Whoever obstructs the right that a third party has over waters; and,
2o.- Whoever, unlawfully and with the purpose of preventing the legitimate use of a person with a right, dams, diverts or detains the waters of rivers, streams, canals or springs, or usurps any right whatsoever relating to the course thereof.
The penalty shall be increased to two years, if in order to commit the offenses expressed in numbers 1o. and 2o. of this article, dikes, locks, gates or other similar works, made in rivers, streams, springs, reservoirs, canals or aqueducts are broken or altered.

Concordancias:

•	CODIGO CIVIL (

## LIBRO II)

, Arts. 612
•	LEY DE AGUAS, Arts. 8, 70, 77, 78

###### Art. 582

Whoever fraudulently removes or diverts waters from the public or from private individuals, whether to take advantage of them for his own benefit, or for any other purpose, shall be punished with imprisonment of eight days to six months and a fine of five to twenty-five dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 169 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

Concordancias:

•	LEY DE AGUAS, Arts. 35, 70, 77, 78

#### CAPITULO VIII

Of usury and pawnshops

###### Art. 583

A loan is usurious in which, directly or indirectly, an interest greater than that permitted by law, or other usurious advantages, is stipulated.

Concordancias:

, Arts. 2102, 2108, 2109, 2110, 2115

•	Gaceta Judicial, USURA, 13-mar-1934
•	Gaceta Judicial, PACTO USURARIO, 04-dic-1934

###### Art. 584

Whoever engages in usurious loans shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to two years and a fine of sixteen to three hundred eleven dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 170 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 585

Whoever conceals, with any other contractual form, the reality of a usurious loan shall be punished with imprisonment of two to four years and a fine of one thousand dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 171 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.
Note: Article amended by Law No. 70, published in Official Register Supplement 427 of December 29, 2006.

###### Art. 586

Whoever, being engaged in the business of loans on pledges, salaries, or wages, does not keep books, recording in them, without blanks or interlineations, the amounts lent, the terms and interest, the names and addresses of those who receive the loan, the nature, quality and value of the objects given in pledge and the other circumstances required by the regulations or ordinances on the matter; or is convicted of falsity in the entries of said books, shall be punished with imprisonment of two to four years and a fine of five hundred dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 172 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.
Note: Article amended by Law No. 70, published in Official Register Supplement 427 of December 29, 2006.

###### Art. 587

The lender who does not give a receipt for the pledge or security received, shall be punished with a fine of double to five times its value and the fulfillment of the penalty may not be suspended.

#### CAPITULO IX

General Provisions

###### Art. 588

The following are exempt from criminal liability and subject only to civil liability, for thefts, robberies with force against things, frauds, or damages that they reciprocally cause each other:

1o.- Spouses, ascendants, descendants or relatives by affinity, in the same line;
2o.- The widowed spouse, with respect to things belonging to his deceased spouse, as long as they have not passed into the power of another; and,
3o.- Brothers and brothers-in-law, if they live together.

The exception of this article is not applicable to strangers, if they participate in the offense.

###### Art. 589

In the offenses dealt with in this Title, with the exception of those detailed in Chapter VIII, the perpetrators may be placed under the special surveillance of the authority, for at least two years and at most five.

###### Art. 590.- Whoever has fraudulently falsified or adulterated keys shall be

sentenced to imprisonment of three months to two years and a fine of eight to thirty-one dollars of the United States of North America.

If the guilty party is a locksmith by trade, he shall be punished with imprisonment of one to three years and a fine of eight to thirty-one dollars of the United States of North America.

Note: Article amended by Art. 173 of Law No. 75, published in Official Register 635 of August 7, 2002.

###### Art. 591.- Public roads are those whose use is public

Concordancias:
•	CODIGO CIVIL (

## LIBRO II)

, Arts. 604


This excerpt sits under: CODIGO PENAL > LIBRO IV)

###### Art. 592

Robbery committed during the night is that executed in the period between seven o'clock past midday and five o'clock in the morning.

###### Art. 593

Every building, apartment, dwelling, hut, cabin, even if movable, or any other place that serves as a dwelling is deemed an inhabited house.

###### Art. 594

The yards, corrals, gardens and any other enclosed lands are deemed dependencies of an inhabited house, as well as the granaries, stables, and any other buildings contained therein, whatever their use, even when they form a separate enclosure within the general enclosure.

###### Art. 595

Mobile pens, intended to contain livestock in the fields, in whatever manner they are made, are deemed a dependency of an inhabited house when they are established on the same space of land with movable cabins or other shelters intended for the guardians.

###### Art. 596

By violence is understood acts of physical coercion exercised upon persons.

By threats is understood means of moral coercion that instill fear of imminent harm.

###### Art. 597.- Force upon things or breaking consists of any breaking,

rupture, demolition, boring, or any other violence executed upon vessels, wagons, aerostats, walls, floors, ceilings, doors, windows, grilles, wardrobes, dressers, chests, suitcases, filing cabinets or any other closed furniture; the removal of chains, bars or other instruments that serve to close, or prevent passage and guard things; and the breaking of straps, ropes, cords or other ties that protect some effects, and the use of lock picks.

, Arts. 1472

•	Gaceta Judicial, FUERZA EN LAS COSAS, 29-abr-1997

###### Art. 598.- Assimilated to theft with force upon things is

The theft of the movables referred to in the preceding article; and, That committed by means of breaking of seals.

###### Art. 599.- Climbing is qualified as

Any entry into houses, yards, corrals, or any other building, gardens, parks and enclosures, executed over doors, roofs, walls, or any other kind of enclosure; and,
Entry through an underground opening, or through balconies or windows, or through any other part not intended for lawful entry.

###### Art. 600.- Lock picks are qualified as

Any hook, clasp, master key, imitated, falsified or altered key;
Keys that have not been intended by the owner, lessee, innkeeper or hotel keeper for the locks, padlocks or bolts to which the guilty party has applied them; and,
Lost, misplaced or stolen keys that have been used to commit the act.

###### Art. 601

A gang is the gathering of three or more persons, with the same criminal intention, for the commission of a crime.

###### Art. 602

By the word weapon is understood any machine, or any other cutting, piercing or blunt instrument that has been taken to kill, wound or strike, even when it is not used.
